## WHAT THIS IS
`sandbox-pokebot/` is the working build of a Pokémon Emerald bot. Its **Story mode** plays the game
autonomously from Littleroot. `40cakes repo/` is a pristine upstream mirror (never edit);
`custom-pokebot/` is the promoted fork. **All work happens in `sandbox-pokebot/`.**

‼ **CLAUDE SUBAGENTS ARE STANDING-APPROVED** for research and assistance (user directive
2026-08-12, in `CLAUDE.md`). Use them freely — fan out on different ANGLES in ONE message, include
one explicitly ADVERSARIAL, tell them what is already ruled out, and keep working while they run.
**Their output is a NOTE, never truth**: verify the load-bearing claim in our own harness. That is
how KI-58 was cracked after four of my own models failed.

## 📚 ARCHIVED SESSIONS (§Ch18, §Ch19, promotion/roster, KI-56, hardening) — DETAIL in `28-HANDOFF-ARCHIVE.md`
⛔ **TEN `##` STUBS MERGED HERE 2026-08-17 (`#24-handoffbudget`) — nothing deleted.** The detail has lived in the archive since 2026-08-13; every DISTINCT lesson the stubs kept is below. What went was ten headings and the fact that the two rules below were each written out three and two times, in separate sections.
1. ⛔⛔ **A CHAPTER'S ACCEPTANCE TEST MUST INCLUDE "AND THE BOT CAN CONTINUE"** — a REAL `calculate_path` off the end tile, never a "controllable" flag. **§Ch15 ended four floors underground, §Ch18 on an eleven-tile island, §Ch19 in a living room, and ALL THREE PASSED THEIR OWN TESTS.** KI-56's prevention rule, violated again by §Ch18 the next day. Every §Ch20 driver carries it. Read it before writing any chapter's acceptance test.
2. ⛔⛔ **WHEN N OF YOUR OWN INSTRUMENTS AGREE ON SOMETHING IMPOSSIBLE, THEY SHARE A MISSING PRIMITIVE.** Four agreed the Mossdeep Gym had no route to its own leaders; all four modelled it as *walk + warp* and it has a **third movement primitive** (a rotating-tile statue puzzle). Even the model-free brute-force walker was wrong — it *changed the map while exploring it*.

# HOW TO RUN / TEST

### Design v2 steps 8-9b — the 2026-09-29 gates and live arms
```
cd tools/story-dev
PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 probe_r25_catch_cap.py      # step 8: the cap of 3 + the rare exemption (K/A rows, 13 mutants)
PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 probe_stock_topoff.py       # step 9a: the ball top-off (L/A/E rows, 20 mutants)
# the 9a live arms: the seed-1 storyresume on r20_badge1_party1_gym.ss1, --set-balls 5 --frames 60000, with
# POKEBOT_R23_BALLRESTOCK=<unset|observe|1> (the exact command heads evidence/2026-09-29-ballrestock-M0.txt)
PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 probe_stock_topoff_sootopolis.py   # step 9b: the Sootopolis consumer (F/K/O/P/S/A/D rows, 39 mutants)
# the 9b live arms: the seed-1 wrapper, --driver earlybasket --frames 16000 on post_hm07.ss1 (EB_EXPECT=buy|ballfail|budget)
# or on its own EB_SAVE_AFTER output (EB_EXPECT=balls|nothing); each arm's exact env heads
# evidence/2026-09-29-ballrestock9b-<ARM>.txt
```

### RULING 20 Phase 2 + `#27-enroutejoin` — the 2026-09-28 drivers (all flags default OFF)
Pair live A/B arms by SEEDING the bot's own RNG (the emulator replays; the bot's organic `random` draws do not — `08`,
2026-09-28). ⛔ If a batch is running, build and drive on a fresh worktree branch cut from `main`.
```
cd tools/story-dev
W="import random,runpy,sys; random.seed(1); sys.argv=['story_harness.py']+sys.argv[1:]; runpy.run_path('story_harness.py', run_name='__main__')"
# P2-b (open fence): the first catch that JOINS the party is Aron at f218453
POKEBOT_R20_OPENFENCE=1 PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 -c "$W" r20_badge1_party1_gym.ss1 --driver storyresume --set-balls 30 --frames 240000 --profile a --log a.log
# #27-enroutejoin: a walk catch derails the Surf hunt under POKEBOT_ENROUTE_FAULT_NOWALK=1; the fix refuses it
R101_ARM=healed101 R101_FENCE=real POKEBOT_R20_OPENFENCE=1 PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 -c "$W" norman_done.ss1 --driver r101hunt --set-balls 20 --frames 45000 --profile b --log b.log
# P2-c (hunt first): the f140752 Abra is refused while the pre-gym-1 counter hunt is armed
POKEBOT_R20_TEAM6=1 POKEBOT_R20_HUNTFIRST=1 PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 -c "$W" r20_pregym1_party4_r116.ss1 --driver huntfirst --frames 60000 --profile c --log c.log
# v2 step 10a (HK at Rustboro), arm K-ON: the kit rides the post-badge-1 heal. Drop BALLRESTOCK for K-ONLY; the
# seven arms' exact flags head evidence/2026-09-29-kittopoff10a-<ARM>.txt; gate: probe_kit_topoff.py
POKEBOT_R23_BALLRESTOCK=1 POKEBOT_R26_KITTOPOFF=1 POKEBOT_R27_KEYITEMS=1 PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 -c "$W" r20_badge1_party1_gym.ss1 --driver storyresume --set-balls 5 --frames 60000 --log k.log
# v2 step 10b (HK at Sootopolis), arm S-ON: the kit rides RULING 18's League-basket visit; the twelve graded arms'
# and the four ballfail arms' exact flags and fixtures head evidence/2026-09-30-kittopoff10b-<ARM>.txt and
# -REVIEW-RERUN.txt; gate: probe_kit_topoff_sootopolis.py
EB_EXPECT=buy POKEBOT_R23_BALLRESTOCK=1 POKEBOT_R26_KITTOPOFF=1 POKEBOT_R27_KEYITEMS=1 PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 -c "$W" saves/post_hm07.ss1 --driver earlybasket --frames 16000 --log s.log
```

### Design v2 steps 11 (M1, the damage-calculator audit) and 12 (W, weaken-to-catch) — the 2026-09-30 gates, live arms and offline audit
```
cd tools/story-dev
PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 probe_damage_calc_decomp.py   # M1a: the decomp-ordered port vs the calculator (45 rows, 38 cases; ~72 s)
PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 probe_dmgaudit.py             # the audit classifier + production pins (191 rows, 220 mutants; ~8 s)

# live arms ($W, post_mtpyre, 90000 frames): OFF unset; ON =1; FAULT =1 + POKEBOT_R25_FAULT_DMGAUDIT_BIDE0=1 (M1B-GRADE.txt heads them; KI-112: run twice, apart)
POKEBOT_R25_DMGAUDIT=1 PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 -c "$W" saves/post_mtpyre.ss1 --driver storyresume --frames 90000 --log m1b.log
# a divergence: run the BASE code's modules in THIS worktree: run_with_base_modules.py <base> <path>[,<path>] <harness args>

POKEBOT_R20_TEAM6=1 POKEBOT_R25_DMGAUDIT=1 POKEBOT_R25_WEAKEN=1 PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 -c "$W" saves/r20_pregym1_party4_r116.ss1 --driver storyresume --frames 240000 --set-balls 30 --set-lead-move "1=Seismic Toss" --log w.log
# v2 step 14 (the batch A/B, T1): gate repro_x4_headless.py (sections F-H); from the MAIN checkout only (a worktree is refused): x4_headless.py --build sandbox --lanes 16 --minutes 780 --arms arms/batch14.json --profile-tag <a NEW tag>; the NIGHT run is detached (no job; survives this session): powershell -File launch_x4h_detached.ps1 -Tag <tag> -Minutes 780; --status <batch dir> is read-only; stop = end its python.exe (its job ends the lanes). GRADING (T2-T4, after the batch ends; a bare stamp resolves under the MAIN checkout's x4-runs from any directory): compare_arms.py <stamp> --json <out>; batch14_checks.py <stamp> --json <out>; batch14_tables.py <stamp> --m1b-out <file> --json <out>; their gates probe_compare_arms.py and probe_batch14_checks.py
```

cd tools/story-dev

PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 run_gates.py        # THE STANDING SUITE — all of it (it prints its own count), exits non-zero on any

PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 grade_ki93_drive.py <debug-log> <harness-log>
#   ⭐ GRADES A `KI-93` DRIVE BY ITS SEQUENCE, NOT ITS TOTALS. Built because counting
#   [KI93-DISMISS]/[KI93-REPICK] lines produced a confident "driven clean" over a real defect.
#   C0 control fired · C1 `KI-47` stops == 0 · C2 every re-pick has a real refusal behind it,
#   keyed on the mon's personality_value · C3 the fix actually engaged.
#   Produce a log with:  POKEBOT_KI93_CENSUS=1 PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 story_harness.py \
#       post_league_shop.ss1 --driver ch24probe --frames 200000 --log <harness-log>
#   then point the grader at sandbox-pokebot/profiles/<profile>/debug-logs/<newest BY NAME>.log
#   ‼ Pick the log by its FILENAME timestamp, never `ls -t` — a still-running drive has the later
#     mtime, which is how the wrong run's numbers got read once already.
#   ⛔ Read its exit code WITHOUT a pipe. Fault matrix: 234031 -> INDETERMINATE(1),
#     232310 -> VACUOUS(1), 235815 -> PASS(0).
PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 probe_chapter_order.py --build custom   # ‼ also check the OTHER fork
PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 probe_stale_citations.py   # ‼ RUN THIS BEFORE TRUSTING ANY `file.py:NNN`
                                          #   IN THE DOCS. 22 were stale on 2026-08-16; it took
                                          #   them to 0. NOT a gate (years of citations would
                                          #   make it red on arrival). Rule: RE-DERIVE, NEVER
                                          #   RE-TYPE — and the durable claim is the SHAPE,
                                          #   not the line, because lines drift on every edit.
PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 story_harness.py post_dive_ki76.ss1 --driver ki76warp --frames 2500000
#   KI-76's live confirmation. ⛔ DO NOT RE-RUN to "check" it — CONFIRMED and committed.

PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 probe_abort_swallowing.py --build custom  # KI-81 ratchet: baselines are EQUAL AGAIN -> {sandbox: 23, custom: 23}. sandbox went 36 -> 31 -> 23 as (b) then (c) landed, and custom followed 36 -> 23 when the file was PROMOTED 2026-08-24 (user-approved). Both forks now read ZERO AST abort-swallowers.

#     a doc with PowerShell; use the editor or Python with encoding='utf-8'. Appending with

# --- §Ch24 PREP: THE GRIND — ⛔⛔ RE-POINTED 2026-09-17. **THE FIXTURE BELOW WAS `post_training.ss1` → `post_grind_L60.ss1`, AND THAT IS A DEAD BRANCH** (ruled `06-DECISIONS-LOG.md` 2026-09-17): it skipped the whole §Ch24 prep chain, so it has NO Ice Beam and NO Fly. **USE `post_ch24ready.ss1`** — already prepped, and `ch24grind` now WALKS itself from the Poké Center (27,49) in through the Victory Road door to the grind tile (15,39).

# --- ⭐⭐⭐ §Ch24 THE WHOLE CHAPTER AS ONE CHAIN (`run_the_gauntlet`) — DRIVEN AND PASSING 2026-09-25 ---
CH24_ENTRY_FLOOR=1 PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 -u story_harness.py post_ch24_atdoor_L75.ss1 --driver ch24gauntlet --frames 600000 --log evidence/<date>-ch24gauntlet.log > evidence/<date>-ch24gauntlet.stdout.txt 2>&1
#   Expect `ACCEPTANCE: PASS`, 7 readings (G-PREPARED/ENTERED/PAIR1/PAIR2/PAIR3/ATDOOR/CLEARED), `missing=[]`,
#     `whiteouts=0`, `failure=None`, `heal_ready=True`, **`battles=5`** (fewer = a fight was SKIPPED).
#     `latch_calls=2` is CORRECT at FLOOR=1 (`train_for_league` no-ops); at production `floor=65` it is 3.
#   ⛔⛔ IT CLEARS THE GAME, crossing the point of no return TWICE. The `finally` RESTORES the fixture ⇒ GIVE IT A BUDGET IT CAN FINISH IN (a frame-capped run never resumes the generator, so no restore fires).
#   ‼ USE `-u`: the harness ends in `set_manual_mode()` and never exits, so if you stop it, block-buffered
#     stdout loses its last few KB — exactly where the verdict lives. VACUITY ARM: run it from a CLEARED save.

# --- §Ch24 THE BRIDGE **IN PRODUCTION** (`enter_the_league`) — DRIVEN AND PASSING 2026-09-25 ---
CH24_ENTRY_FLOOR=1 PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 story_harness.py post_ch24_atdoor_L75.ss1 --driver ch24bridge --frames 300000 --log evidence/<date>-ch24bridge.log
#   Runs BOTH legs (`prepare_and_open_the_league` -> `enter_the_league`) because the SEAM is what is under test. Expect: `ACCEPTANCE: PASS`, 9 readings, `missing=[]`, `whiteouts=0`, `next_leg_premise_ok=True`, `latch_calls=2`, `engage_signal='victory_flag'`, `room_at=(6,7)`, and a `fixture RESTORED` line. ⚠ FLOOR=1 is NOT production's 65.
#   VACUITY ARM (must REFUSE with `readings=[]`): the same command from `post_sidney_L75.ss1`.
#   ⛔⛔ THIS RUN CROSSES THE POINT OF NO RETURN and sets `ENTERED_ELITE_FOUR` PERMANENTLY. Give it a budget it can FINISH in — a frame-capped run never resumes the generator, so the `finally` that restores the fixture never fires and `current_state.ss1` keeps a COMMITTED state.
# --- §Ch24 THE REPEATING UNIT **IN PRODUCTION** (`fight_one_room`) — DRIVEN AND PASSING 2026-09-24 ---
#   CH24_ROOM_FROM=sidney PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 story_harness.py post_sidney_L75.ss1 \
#       --driver ch24room --frames 60000 --log evidence/<date>-ch24room.log
#   ⛔⛔ NEVER `post_sidney_door_open.ss1` — same map/tile, but the PRE-CLIMB party at 17% HP with 5
#     FAINTED; a loss from it reads as a port defect.
#   ✅ ALL THREE PAIRS DRIVEN AND PASSING 2026-09-24 — each its OWN fixture, each its own env value:
#     CH24_ROOM_FROM=sidney  post_sidney_L75.ss1  -> PHOEBE, ends PHOEBES_ROOM (6,6)
#     CH24_ROOM_FROM=phoebe  post_phoebe_L75.ss1  -> GLACIA, ends GLACIAS_ROOM (6,6)
#     CH24_ROOM_FROM=glacia  post_glacia_L75.ss1  -> DRAKE,  ends DRAKES_ROOM  (6,6)
#   ⛔ ENV VALUE AND FIXTURE MUST MATCH (`CH24_ROOM_FROM` names the room you START in) — a mismatch
#     IS the premise arm and refuses with `readings=[]`. ✅ **CORRECTED 2026-09-25: these three are ALSO
#     driven as ONE continuous chain by `--driver ch24gauntlet` above** ⇒ basket use across a single bag
#     is now MEASURED. *(superseded: "THREE INDEPENDENT LEGS, NOT ONE GAUNTLET — basket use across a
#     continuous run is UNMEASURED".)*
#   Expect on any of the three: `ACCEPTANCE: PASS`, 9 readings, `missing=[]`, `whiteouts=0`,
#     `heal_ready=True`, `victory_flag_set=True`, `exit_door_live={(6,1):0,(6,2):0}`.
#   ARMS: POKEBOT_CH24ROOM_FAULT=noengage | healnoop  (+ the premise arm above).
#   ‼ ~14,000 frames is a full pass; the 60,000 cap exists so a FAILING run still prints its verdict
#     rather than idling to a huge cap (a frame-capped run never resumes the generator, so the
#     driver's `finally` — and its fixture RESTORE — never fire).
# --- §Ch24 THE REPEATING UNIT AS **HARNESS DRIVERS** (rooms 1->2, 2->3, 3->4) — ALL THREE DRIVEN
#     AND PASSING 2026-09-17, 📦 ARCHIVED 2026-09-24 (R3 budget) to `28-HANDOFF-ARCHIVE.md` under
#     "§Ch24 — the three harness-driver room recipes": every command, capture var, fault arm and
#     `Expect:` block verbatim (ONLY the title gained "AS HARNESS DRIVERS"). ⇒ RE-VERIFY from the
#     archive; for FORWARD work use the PRODUCTION recipe above.
#   ‼ TWO FACTS TOO EXPENSIVE TO LOSE BEHIND A POINTER: ⛔ READ `ACCEPTANCE:`, NEVER THE LAST LINE —
#     every driver ends `HANG CONFIRMED` on dropping to Manual (the 20,000-frame watchdog, not a
#     defect). ⚠ `hp_entering_<room>` IS A FAKE READING — a driver is SUSPENDED across the fight, so
#     "before" is sampled AFTER. ⭐ That same suspension is what made `battle_is_active()` unusable
#     in the production leg (see the `➡➡➡` block).
# --- §Ch24 LEG B (the ENDGAME TAIL, `ch24tailprobe`) + the WARP-CHAIN probe — 📦 ARCHIVED 2026-09-25 (R3 budget) to `28-HANDOFF-ARCHIVE.md` under "§Ch24 — the endgame-tail and warp-chain recipes", VERBATIM. ⛔ IRREVERSIBLE and OBSERVE-ONLY; its ACCEPTANCE is INSTRUMENT HEALTH, not a verdict. ⭐ READ IT BEFORE BUILDING THE CHAMPION LEG — it is the only end-to-end measurement of the tail.
#   ✅ **THE POST-VICTORY STALL IS SOLVED AND MEASURED — NOT A LIVE BLOCKER.** `B` clears the msgbox (control in **297** frames; the no-press CONTROL `POKEBOT_E4DISMISS_FAULT_NOPRESS=1` burns its full 2,600-frame budget and never recovers), and the walk out reaches **`HALL1 (5,12)`** in **164** frames (`POKEBOT_E4EXIT_FAULT_NOWALK=1` stays put, continuation REFUSED). *(These three lines read “‼ WHAT *IS* A DEFECT: it then sits 1,800 frames in `wait_until_truly_controllable` … That is the first thing §Ch24 leg 1 hits” — true when written, superseded the same day. ‼ A Tier 2 re-audit found it: I updated the banner and the punch list and left the RUN RECIPES a session behind, which is a P3 break committed while documenting P3 breaks.)* ⛔ `#24-e4postbattlestall` is `[~]` — what remains is the WIRING, and only SIDNEY's room is measured. ⛔⛔ **BOTH COMMANDS ABOVE START FROM `post_sidney_door_open.ss1`, WHICH IS CORRECT FOR *THESE* PROBES (they grade on control and position) AND WRONG FOR THE LEG — `DEFEATED_ELITE_4_SIDNEY` IS ALREADY TRUE THERE. The leg starts from `post_ch24_atdoor.ss1`.**
POKEBOT_KI93_CENSUS=1 PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 story_harness.py post_league_shop.ss1 \
    --driver ch24probe --frames 200000 --log ki93.log
PYTHONUTF8=1 py -3.12 grade_ki93_drive.py <newest debug-log BY FILENAME> ki93.log
#   The `KI-93` drive + its SEQUENCE grader (C0 control / C1 livelock / C2 every re-pick justified /

⭐ **THE SUITE IS SAFE TO RUN BARE AS OF 2026-09-08** — `run_gates.py` and every REGISTERED gate reconfigure stdout to UTF-8, and the runner launches its children with `PYTHONUTF8=1`. Three gates used to exit 1 with a `UnicodeEncodeError` on a default Windows cp1252 console **after their checks had passed**, so they looked like real failures.
⛔ **When you claim a tool works bare, TEST IT BARE** — the settling command is
`env -u PYTHONUTF8 PYTHONIOENCODING=cp1252 py -3.12 run_gates.py --quick` (must exit 0).
